## OpenZeppelin zkSync Layer 1 Audit

- Report: [2022_09-openzeppelin-zksync-layer-1-audit.md](<reports/2022_09-openzeppelin-zksync-layer-1-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2022-09-30
- Description: OpenZeppelin audit of the zkSync 2.0 layer 1 Diamond-proxy rollup contracts (DiamondProxy, Executor, Mailbox, DiamondCut, Getters, Governance facets and supporting libraries) at a zksync-2-dev commit, with the Verifier and priority mode out of scope. Fixes for the four Medium and other findings were made in private zksync-2-dev commits.

## zkSync Era System Contracts contest Findings &amp; Analysis Report (Code4rena)

- Report: [2023_03-code4rena-zksync-era-system-contracts.md](<reports/2023_03-code4rena-zksync-era-system-contracts.md>)
- Auditor: Code4rena
- Date: 2023-06-14
- Description: Code4rena contest (10 to 19 March 2023) on the 40 zkSync Era L2 system contracts, libraries and interfaces (2,418 SLOC) in the contest repository, with the bootloader explicitly out of scope. One High (MsgValueSimulator self-call) and five Medium findings were reported, with no fix commits named.

## zkSync Fee Model and Token Bridge Audit

- Report: [2023_03-openzeppelin-zksync-fee-model-token-bridge-audit.md](<reports/2023_03-openzeppelin-zksync-fee-model-token-bridge-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-03-09
- Description: OpenZeppelin audit (23 January to 17 February 2023) of the zkSync Era L1/L2 ERC-20 token bridge contracts and of system-contracts pull request #170, covering the new bootloader fee model (diff) and several system contracts. One High finding (operator refund overflow in the bootloader) and five Medium findings were reported, with fix commits listed for most issues.

## MatterLabs zkSync Era Zero Knowledge Security Audit

- Report: [2023_04-halborn-zksync-era-circuits-audit.md](<reports/2023_04-halborn-zksync-era-circuits-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Halborn
- Date: 2023-04-04
- Description: Halborn audit (January to March 2023) of the zkSync Era zero-knowledge circuits in sync_vm (VM, glue circuits such as log sorting, storage validity and demultiplexing, precompiles, scheduler and recursion) and of the L1 PLONK verifier contracts. It found one Critical completeness bug in the storage validity circuit, which was fixed, plus minor issues.

## zkSync Upgrade System Audit

- Report: [2023_06-openzeppelin-zksync-upgrade-system-audit.md](<reports/2023_06-openzeppelin-zksync-upgrade-system-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-06-30
- Description: OpenZeppelin diff audit of system-contracts PR #272 and zksync-2-contracts PR #143, which introduce a forced L2 upgrade transaction type and move bootloader/default-account/verifier updates from the Governance facet into a time-delayed DefaultUpgrade contract. One High finding (unset L2 upgrade block number blocking future upgrades) was reported in the L1 upgrade and Executor code, and no issue was marked resolved in the report.

- Report: [2023_09-openzeppelin-zksync-l1messenger-upgrade-audit.md](<reports/2023_09-openzeppelin-zksync-l1messenger-upgrade-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-09-14
- Description: OpenZeppelin diff audit of system-contracts PR #283 and zksync-2-contracts PR #165, which split system and user logs, add L1Messenger rolling hashes and Compressor-verified compressed state diffs and bytecode pubdata, and move related checks into the L1 Executor. ## zkSync Era Findings &amp; Analysis Report

- Report: [2023_10-code4rena-zksync-era.md](<reports/2023_10-code4rena-zksync-era.md>)
- Auditor: Code4rena
- Date: 2024-02-29
- Description: Code4rena competitive audit (October 2-23, 2023) of zkSync Era&#x27;s L1 contracts, L2 system contracts, bootloader, Yul precompiles, L2 bridges and the boojum-based zkEVM circuits. It reports 6 High findings, all in the circuits (main VM and log sorter), plus Medium and QA issues.

## zkSync Keccak256 Upgrade Audit

- Report: [2023_10-openzeppelin-zksync-keccak256-upgrade-audit.md](<reports/2023_10-openzeppelin-zksync-keccak256-upgrade-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-10-27
- Description: OpenZeppelin audit of era-system-contracts PR #41, which introduces a more efficient keccak256 precompile (padding moved into the circuit) and the bootloader/ContractDeployer logic to upgrade to it. Only Low and Note issues were found, all resolved in follow-up PRs.

## ZkSync snark-wrapper Security Review

- Report: [2023_11-spearbit-zksync-snark-wrapper-audit.md](<reports/2023_11-spearbit-zksync-snark-wrapper-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Spearbit
- Date: 2023-11-28
- Description: Spearbit review of the snark-wrapper constraints (with the Poseidon2 circuit gadgets in rescue-poseidon) that encode a Boojum STARK verifier inside the final PLONK compression SNARK, checked for faithful translation of the native era-boojum and rescue-poseidon verifier code. No translation errors were found; two Low issues concern public-input aggregation.

### Repository: <a href="https://github.com/matter-labs/snark-wrapper"><code>matter-labs/snark-wrapper</code></a>

## December Diff and Governance Audit

- Report: [2023_12-openzeppelin-zksync-december-diff-governance-audit.md](<reports/2023_12-openzeppelin-zksync-december-diff-governance-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-12-22
- Description: Diff audit of zkSync Era L1/L2 contracts (era-contracts c44852f to 2e0734b) and system contracts (era-system-contracts 4dca36d to 0b238cd), covering the AllowList removal, ValidatorTimelock timestamp packing and L2StandardERC20 reinitialization. New contracts (Governance timelock, BaseZkSyncUpgrade/DefaultUpgrade, LibMap) and ValidatorTimelock were audited in full.

## Short-Term Fee Model Changes Audit

- Report: [2023_12-openzeppelin-zksync-short-term-fee-model-changes-audit.md](<reports/2023_12-openzeppelin-zksync-short-term-fee-model-changes-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-12-13
- Description: Audit of the zkSync Era short-term fee model changes in the L1 diamond facets, interfaces and TransactionValidator (era-contracts 2e0734b) and in the bootloader, Compressor, L1Messenger and IMailbox system contracts (era-system-contracts 0b238cd). The changes make the operator supply L2 gas and pubdata prices and add governor-configurable fee parameters for L1-to-L2 transactions.

- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-03-22
- Description: Audit of the ZK governance token (ZkTokenV1, an upgradeable ERC-20 with permit and vote delegation), the ZkCappedMinter and the airdrop ZkMerkleDistributor in the zk-governance repository at commit 08ec4e7. The contracts handle minting and initial distribution of the ZKsync token.

## ZKsync State Transition Diff Audit

- Report: [2024_03-openzeppelin-zksync-state-transition-diff-audit.md](<reports/2024_03-openzeppelin-zksync-state-transition-diff-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-03-27
- Description: Diff audit of the ZK Stack state-transition L1 contracts in era-contracts between base f29f2b7 and head d5c5d7a, covering the new StateTransitionManager, the generalized ValidatorTimelock, DiamondInit/DiamondProxy/storage and the Admin, Executor, Getters, Mailbox and base facets. The changes enable deploying and managing multiple ZK Chains with Validium mode and custom base tokens.

## Decentralized Governance Audit

- Report: [2024_04-openzeppelin-decentralized-governance-audit.md](<reports/2024_04-openzeppelin-decentralized-governance-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-04-26
- Description: Audit of the L1 governance contracts of zk-governance (ProtocolUpgradeHandler, SecurityCouncil, Guardians, Multisig, EmergencyUpgradeBoard) at a3e361d and c66fc0b, plus diff audits of the related era-contracts changes (StateTransitionManager, ValidatorTimelock, Admin/Mailbox facets, bridges and Bridgehub) from a86cf76 to 189a7fe. The upgrade decentralizes ZKsync Era protocol upgrades and adds freeze/emergency-upgrade incident response.

## ZK Stack VM1.5 Diff Audit

- Report: [2024_04-openzeppelin-zk-stack-vm1.5-diff-audit.md](<reports/2024_04-openzeppelin-zk-stack-vm1.5-diff-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-04-08
- Description: Diff audit of the ZKsync VM1.5 changes in era-contracts between base f29f2b7 and head 705a4c8, covering the redesigned pubdata gas accounting in the bootloader and system contracts, the new GasBoundCaller, CodeOracle and P256Verify contracts, MsgValueSimulator stipends, and support for 6 blobs in the L1 Executor. New contracts were reviewed in full and the rest for the diff.

## ZKsync L1 Governance Diff Audit

- Report: [2024_08-openzeppelin-zksync-l1-governance-diff-audit.md](<reports/2024_08-openzeppelin-zksync-l1-governance-diff-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-08-08
- Description: Diff audit of the ZKsync L1 governance contracts (ProtocolUpgradeHandler, Guardians, SecurityCouncil, EmergencyUpgradeBoard, Multisig and interfaces) in zksync-association/zk-governance from base 8456ffb to head b2e0143, covering the legal veto period, Guardians L2 proposal powers and freeze changes. Follow-up diff audits reviewed commit 25fcd9c and pull requests #3 and #4 (August 2024).

## zkSync Era Security Audit: Bug Fixes Review

- Report: [2024_09-chainlight-zksync-era-bug-fixes-review.md](<reports/2024_09-chainlight-zksync-era-bug-fixes-review.md>)
- Auditor: ChainLight
- Date: 2024-09-24
- Description: ChainLight review of recently reported soundness bugs and their fixes in the zkSync Era Boojum circuits and crypto libraries (SHA256, UInt32/UInt8 gadgets, Poseidon2 parameters, permutation argument, recursive verifier transcript, gate metadata, mixed point addition), analysing root causes, variants and remediations. Four High bugs (BUGFIX-001 to BUGFIX-004) were confirmed patched; the remaining Low/Informational issues were patched, mitigated or left as Won&#x27;t Fix.

## FFLONK Verifier Audit

- Report: [2024_09-openzeppelin-zksync-fflonk-verifier-audit.md](<reports/2024_09-openzeppelin-zksync-fflonk-verifier-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-09-26
- Description: OpenZeppelin three-phase audit (July to September 2024) of the Yul FFLONK proof verifier (FflonkYul.sol) intended to replace the ZKsync L1 PLONK verifier, plus a review of the Executor proving flow and verifier interfaces in era-contracts for compatibility. One Critical proof-forgery issue caused by unvalidated calldata array lengths was found in phase 1 and resolved.

### Repository: <a href="https://github.com/matter-labs/fflonk-verifier"><code>matter-labs/fflonk-verifier</code></a>

## Protocol Defense Audit

- Report: [2024_09-openzeppelin-zksync-protocol-defense-audit-final.md](<reports/2024_09-openzeppelin-zksync-protocol-defense-audit-final.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-09-09
- Description: Diff audit of era-contracts pull request #524 at commit 1957571 (custom errors, stricter solhint rules, OpenZeppelin v5 support, floating pragmas and gas optimizations) across L1, L2, gas-bound-caller and system contracts including the bootloader. A follow-up diff audit covered the 7ccade5 to 874bc6b changes (ZKsync renaming, clean-up, bootloader upgradeSystemContextIfNeeded removal).

## ZKsync Era CodeHawks Competitive Audit Report

- Report: [2024_10-codehawks-zksync-era.md](<reports/2024_10-codehawks-zksync-era.md>)
- Auditor: CodeHawks
- Date: 2024-12-02
- Description: CodeHawks competitive audit (October 28 - December 2, 2024) of the ZKsync Gateway release era-contracts (L1 bridges, asset router and native token vaults, Bridgehub, chain facets, DA contracts, upgrades, L2 contracts, system contracts, bootloader and precompiles) and the zk-governance L1/L2 contracts in the Cyfrin/2024-10-zksync repository. It reports 1 High (priority operation re-execution when migrating the Era chain from Gateway back to L1), 10 Medium and 18 Low findings.

## ZKsync: ZKChain and Gateway Upgrade Audit

- Report: [2024_10-openzeppelin-zksync-zkchain-gateway-upgrade-audit.md](<reports/2024_10-openzeppelin-zksync-zkchain-gateway-upgrade-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2024-10-24
- Description: Diff audit of era-contracts at commit ef318e21 against 9615d90 (PR #793) covering asset bridging (asset routers, native token vaults, L1Nullifier, legacy bridges), Bridgehub/CTM chain migration to and from the Gateway, state-transition facets and data availability contracts, with several files audited in full. PRs #939 and #948 migrating to custom errors were also audited.

## Trust Security ZKsync Era Contracts Audit

- Report: [2024_10-trust-security-zksync-era-contracts-audit.md](<reports/2024_10-trust-security-zksync-era-contracts-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Trust Security
- Date: 2024-10-13
- Description: Trust Security audit of the ZKsync Era contracts at era-contracts commit 29f9ff4b, covering the L1 bridges, Bridgehub, StateTransitionManager, hyperchain diamond facets and upgrades, L2 bridge contracts, and the system contracts including the bootloader and precompiles. Governance contracts were out of scope; the report lists 3 Medium, 23 Low and 47 recommendations, with team responses still pending.

- Report: [2024_12-audittens-zksync-gateway-audit.md](<reports/2024_12-audittens-zksync-gateway-audit.md>)

All Critical/High findings were fixed in reviewed commits, and the in-scope diff up to a5754174 was briefly reviewed.

## zkSync Era Security Audit: Bug Fixes Review Part 2

- Report: [2025_01-chainlight-zksync-era-bug-fixes-review-part-2.md](<reports/2025_01-chainlight-zksync-era-bug-fixes-review-part-2.md>)
- Auditor: ChainLight
- Date: 2025-01-24
- Description: ChainLight follow-up review of recently reported bugs and their fixes in the zkSync Era Boojum circuits, scoped to the diffs v0.150.20...protocol-v27-rc of zksync-protocol and v0.30.13...crypto-v27-rc of zksync-crypto (non-native field normalize/negated tracking, EVM simulator memory and gas stipends). No Critical/High issues were found; one Medium (BUGFIX2-005, in-circuit stipend not assigned to the callee frame) and one Low were patched.

## ZKChain Release Candidate Audit

- Report: [2025_02-openzeppelin-zkchain-release-candidate-audit-e3dd33c.md](<reports/2025_02-openzeppelin-zkchain-release-candidate-audit-e3dd33c.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-02-10
- Description: Revised edition of the OpenZeppelin ZKChain release candidate diff audit of era-contracts, comparing head e3dd33c against base 79215dc across the bridging, Bridgehub, governance, upgrade, state-transition, DA and system contracts. It additionally covers the 91631aa to e3dd33c changes (Executor and L1GatewayBase updates, IVerifier removal, state-transition file renames); only Low and Note issues were found.

### Repository: <a href="https://github.com/matter-labs/era-contracts"><code>matter-labs/era-contracts</code></a>

## ZKChain Release Candidate Audit

- Report: [2025_02-openzeppelin-zkchain-release-candidate-audit.md](<reports/2025_02-openzeppelin-zkchain-release-candidate-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-02-10
- Description: OpenZeppelin diff audit of the ZKChain/Gateway release candidate in era-contracts from base 79215dc to head 91631aa, covering asset routers, native token vaults, L1Nullifier, Bridgehub, MessageRoot, governance restrictions, upgrade contracts, ChainTypeManager, ZK chain facets, priority tree, DA contracts and several system contracts including the bootloader. The changes mostly address fixes and improvements from previous audits; only Low and Note issues were found.

## FFLONK and EVM Equivalence Diff Audit

- Report: [2025_03-openzeppelin-zksync-fflonk-evm-equivalence-diff-audit.md](<reports/2025_03-openzeppelin-zksync-fflonk-evm-equivalence-diff-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-03-04
- Description: Diff audit of era-contracts at head commit 5731979 against per-file base commits, covering the FFLONK/PLONK dual verifier changes (L1 and L2 verifiers) and the EVM emulator/EVM equivalence changes across L1, L2 and system contracts, with EvmPredeploysManager and the Identity precompile audited in full. No Critical/High findings; fixes were reviewed in PRs #1255, #1257, #1273-#1276 and the final state is at commit f1cae64.

## ZKsync Crypto Precompile Audit

- Report: [2025_04-openzeppelin-zksync-crypto-precompile-audit.md](<reports/2025_04-openzeppelin-zksync-crypto-precompile-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-04-03
- Description: Audit of zksync-crypto pull request #77 at commit ccf5fa3, covering the boojum circuit gadgets (curves, non-native field, tower extensions, algebraic torus, u256/u512) and the native BN256 pairing code in the pairing crate used for the EC precompile circuits. Five High findings were reported and resolved in pull requests #87-#90 (H-02 deemed not an issue).

## Pull Request #1436 Review

- Report: [2025_05-openzeppelin-zksync-v28-pr-review.md](<reports/2025_05-openzeppelin-zksync-v28-pr-review.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-05-02
- Description: Review of era-contracts PR #1436 (v28), which fixes a Matter Labs-reported Medium-severity issue where pending upgrade data left on an inactive settlement layer could block upgrades there and prevent a chain from migrating back from Gateway to L1. The reviewed scope is the PR&#x27;s changes to BaseZkSyncUpgrade, BaseZkSyncUpgradeGenesis and ZkSyncUpgradeErrors, which were confirmed to fix the issue.

## ZKsync V29 Release Audit

- Report: [2025_06-openzeppelin-zksync-v29-release-audit.md](<reports/2025_06-openzeppelin-zksync-v29-release-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-06-26
- Description: Two-phase diff audit of era-contracts against base 7278847: Phase 1 covered the Reduced Interoperability feature (MessageRoot, message verification, Executor/Mailbox facets, bootloader interop-root handling, L2InteropRootStorage) at 3092fc0, and Phase 2 covered Fast Finality pre-commitments, ValidatorTimelock roles, the V29 upgrade contracts and other L1/system-contract changes at 903cfed2. Fixes were reviewed across individual PRs up to the final snapshot babbed5.

## ZKsync OS Audit

- Report: [2025_09-openzeppelin-zksync-os-audit.md](<reports/2025_09-openzeppelin-zksync-os-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2025-09-02
- Description: OpenZeppelin three-phase engagement on ZKsync OS, the Rust state transition function (bootloader, system, system hooks, EE framework, EVM interpreter, oracle provider, runner and forward system), with two high-level assessments followed by a full audit of both scopes at a single commit. Three Critical and one High issues (sequencer DoS via unsupported EE routing, return data buffer draining, usize-dependent non-determinism, legacy transaction access list abuse) were found and resolved.

### Repository: <a href="https://github.com/matter-labs/zksync-os"><code>matter-labs/zksync-os</code></a>

| Revision | Commit date | Audited files or directories |

## ZKsync OS Security Audit

- Report: [2025_10-taran-zksync-os-stf-audit.md](<reports/2025_10-taran-zksync-os-stf-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Taran Space
- Date: 2025-10-15
- Description: Two-phase audit of the ZKsync OS RISC-V state transition function (operation layer crates basic_bootloader, basic_system, evm_interpreter and zk_ee) in matter-labs/zksync-os, first for general safety and EVM compatibility at 96d9d37 and then for L1 integration (messaging, withdrawals, pubdata, upgrades) at 5e69d44 (v0.0.5). The proving layer and Account Abstraction were out of scope; fixes were verified in September 2025.

## Audittens ZKsync OS Security Review

- Report: [2025_11-audittens-zksync-os-v0.0.26-security-review.md](<reports/2025_11-audittens-zksync-os-v0.0.26-security-review.md>)
- Auditor: Audittens
- Date: 2025-11-04
- Description: Audittens security review (June-November 2025) of parts of the ZKsync OS state transition function in matter-labs/zksync-os: bootloader, system, storage model and zk_ee crates at 50dd547, PR #152 (multiblock batch proving, aad3fab), and bootloader, system, EVM interpreter, forward/proof-running system and system-hook crates at c3698e4. The report only summarizes findings by severity (5 Critical, 8 High, of which one High acknowledged) without per-finding details or locations.

## Taran Space Security Audit of ZKsync OS - Crypto

- Report: [2025_12-taran-zksync-os-crypto-audit.md](<reports/2025_12-taran-zksync-os-crypto-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Taran Space
- Date: 2025-12-23
- Description: Taran Space audit (October-November 2025) of the callable_oracles and crypto crates of matter-labs/zksync-os at f32e78b, focusing on ECC and field arithmetic (secp256k1, secp256r1, BN254, BLS12-381, bigint delegation). Two Critical and one High finding were reported in the crypto crate and verified as fixed in December 2025 (fix commits not named).

## Tooling Engagement Report for ZKsync Airbender

- Report: [2026_04-veridise-zksync-airbender-v2-security-assessment.md](<reports/2026_04-veridise-zksync-airbender-v2-security-assessment.md>)
- Auditor: Veridise
- Date: 2026-04-22
- Description: Veridise tooling-based security assessment (February to April 2026) of the ZKsync Airbender RISC-V zkVM prover at commit 5ecb674, combining Picus/LLZK determinism checks of selected constraint-system circuits, fuzzing of the transpiler VM and unrolled prover stages, and targeted manual review of the full-statement verifiers. Three Critical issues (an underconstrained jump/branch/SLT circuit and two verifier-composition flaws) were fixed in pull requests #212 and #258; one Low and two Warnings were acknowledged.

## v.31 Interop Audit

- Report: [2026_05-openzeppelin-zksync-v31-release-audit.md](<reports/2026_05-openzeppelin-zksync-v31-release-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2026-05-15
- Description: Diff audit of era-contracts from base babbed5 to head 4271d0a covering the v31 interoperability (InteropCenter/InteropHandler, ERC-7786), settlement-layer migration, base token and asset tracker changes across L1, DA and system contracts, plus a second diff to head dab8e51 covering the v31 upgrade contracts and PR #2182. It also reviewed zk-governance PRs #36 and #38, with fixes reviewed in individual era-contracts PRs and zk-governance PR #42.

### Repository: <a href="https://github.com/matter-labs/era-contracts"><code>matter-labs/era-contracts</code></a>

### OpenZeppelin zkSync Layer 1 Diff Audit

- Report: [irrelevant/2022_11-openzeppelin-zksync-layer-1-diff-audit.md](<reports/irrelevant/2022_11-openzeppelin-zksync-layer-1-diff-audit.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2022-11-25
- Description: OpenZeppelin diff audit of the zkSync 2.0 layer 1 contracts (facets, libraries and the new AllowList) between two commits of the private matter-labs/zksync-2-dev repository. The entire scope is in the private repository and none of the cited commits are publicly available.

### zkSync Competitive Security Assessment

- Report: [irrelevant/2022_12-secure3-zksync-competitive-security-assessment.md](<reports/irrelevant/2022_12-secure3-zksync-competitive-security-assessment.md>)
- Auditor: Secure3
- Date: 2022-12-05
- Description: Secure3 competitive security assessment (audit contest plus manual and static review) of the zkSync Era Layer 1 contracts (diamond proxy facets, libraries, L1 bridges and allow list) at commit 8bc57b7273a61b04d9ca96b5d3443f5a8f0a150e. The report does not name the repository and the audited commit is not available in any public matter-labs repository, so the scope is inaccessible.

### zkSync GnosisSafeZk Assessment

- Report: [irrelevant/2023_05-openzeppelin-zksync-gnosissafezk-assessment.md](<reports/irrelevant/2023_05-openzeppelin-zksync-gnosissafezk-assessment.md>)
- Auditor: OpenZeppelin
- Date: 2023-05-26
- Description: OpenZeppelin assessment of the GnosisSafeZk and GnosisSafeL2Zk multisig wallet contracts in protofire/safe-contracts, a minor adaptation of Gnosis Safe v1.3.0 for deployment on zkSync Era. The scope is a smart-contract wallet application and not zkSync protocol logic, circuits or prover.

### MatterLabs Verifier Smart Contract Security Assessment

- Report: [irrelevant/2023_09-halborn-matterlabs-verifier-audit.md](<reports/irrelevant/2023_09-halborn-matterlabs-verifier-audit.md>)
- Auditor: Halborn
- Date: 2023-09-15
- Description: Halborn assessment (July 2023) of the new zkSync Era L1 verifier contract, a modified PLONK verifier for the Era proof system, using manual tests of valid and malformed proofs plus Slither and MythX. No issues were found.

### Functional formal verification of ZKsync&#x27;s on-chain verifier

- Report: [irrelevant/2024_10-nethermind-zksync-verifier-formal-verification.md](<reports/irrelevant/2024_10-nethermind-zksync-verifier-formal-verification.md>)
- Auditor: Nethermind
- Description: Presentation slides on Nethermind&#x27;s EasyCrypt mechanized proof of functional correctness of the ZKsync on-chain PLONK SNARK verifier (Yul extraction related to the algebraic specification via pRHL). The slides name no repository, file or commit of the verified source, so no audited source revision can be identified.

### Formal Verification of the ZKSync Verifier: A First for Production zk Systems

- Report: [irrelevant/2025_02-nethermind-zksync-verifier-formal-verification-writeup.md](<reports/irrelevant/2025_02-nethermind-zksync-verifier-formal-verification-writeup.md>)
- Auditor: Nethermind
- Date: 2025-02-07
- Description: Nethermind blog write-up of the EasyCrypt mechanized proof that the Yul extraction of the ZKsync on-chain PLONK verifier conforms to its PLONK/plookup specification. It pins no commit, tag or PR of the verified source (only an illustrative branch link to a contest repository), so no audited source revision can be identified.
